Abstract: Forecasting innovation, intended as the emergence of new technological combinations, is a fundamental challenge for science and policy. We show that forthcoming combinations leave an early trace in the collective language of patents, with predictive signals detectable even decades in advance. We show that signal is not attributable to any single inventor, but emerges as a collective shift in how technologies are described across thousands of patents. To this end, we introduce TechToken, a transformer-based model that treats technologies, classified by International Patent Classification codes, as words in its vocabulary, learning the language of technologies by embedding these codes during fine-tuning. We define context similarity between code embeddings as a measure of linguistic convergence and show that it accurately predicts first technological combinations. TechToken also improves general representation quality, outperforming state-of-the-art models across different patent-related tasks.

</details>


### [111] [BenCSSmark: Making the Social Sciences Count in LLM Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04886)
*Arnault Chatelain,Étienne Ollion,Qianwen Guan,Diandra Fabre,Lorraine Goeuriot,Emile Chapuis,Abdelkrim Beloued,Marie Candito,Nicolas Hervé,Didier Schwab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出社会科学任务在LLM基准测试中代表性不足的问题，并引入BenCSSmark基准来整合计算社会科学家的数据集，以促进更鲁棒、透明且具有社会相关性的AI系统发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM基准测试中社会科学任务严重缺失，而社会科学领域每年产出大量精心标注、上下文敏感的数据集，整合这些数据能显著提升AI模型的泛化能力和鲁棒性，同时反哺社会科学研究。

Method: 提出BenCSSmark基准，整合计算社会科学家的标注数据集，将社会科学视角系统性地纳入LLM评估框架。

Result: BenCSSmark有望促进更鲁棒、透明且具有社会相关性的AI系统，推动社会科学与AI领域的协作。

Conclusion: 将社会科学任务纳入LLM基准测试对AI评估和社会科学研究均具有重要价值，BenCSSmark是这一方向的具体实践。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the under-representation of social science tasks in contemporary LLM benchmarks limits advances in both LLM evaluation and social scientific inquiry. Benchmarks -- standardized tools for assessing computational systems -- are pivotal in the development of artificial intelligence (AI), including large language models (LLMs). Benchmarks do more than measure progress -- they actively structure it, shaping reputations, research agendas, and commercial outcomes. Despite this central role, the social sciences are largely absent from mainstream evaluation frameworks, even though scholars in these fields generate dozens of rigorously annotated, context-sensitive datasets each year. Integrating this work into benchmark design could significantly improve the generalization and robustness of AI models. In turn, models trained on social scientific tasks would likely yield better performance on classic and contemporary tasks in disciplines as diverse as history, sociology, political science or economics. This is all the more pressing as these disciplines are quickly turning to LLMs for assistance. To address this gap, we introduce BenCSSmark, a benchmark composed of datasets annotated by computational social scientists. By integrating social scientific perspectives into benchmarking, BenCSSmark seeks to promote more robust, transparent, and socially relevant AI systems and to foster efficient collaboration.

</details>


### [112] [A Comparative Analysis of Machine Learning and Deep Learning Models for Tweet Sentiment Classification: A Case Study on the Sentiment140 Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04888)
*Vita Anggraini,Cintya Bella,Bastian,Luluk Muthoharoh,Ardika Satria,Martin C. T. Manullang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究在Sentiment140数据集上比较了基于TF-IDF的逻辑回归与BiLSTM深度学习模型的情感分析性能。逻辑回归以73.5%的准确率优于BiLSTM的69.17%，且深度学习模型出现轻微过拟合。最终将模型部署为Streamlit网页应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体爆炸式增长产生了对实时分析非结构化公众情绪的自动化系统的迫切需求。

Method: 在Sentiment140数据集10,000条推文子集上，比较了基于TF-IDF特征的传统逻辑回归模型与双向长短期记忆（BiLSTM）深度学习架构。

Result: 逻辑回归准确率73.5%，BiLSTM准确率69.17%，深度学习模型表现出轻度过拟合。

Conclusion: 对于中等规模非正式文本数据，具有稳健特征提取的经典机器学习方法可以胜过更复杂的深度学习方法。最终将模型集成到Streamlit交互式Web应用中并部署在Hugging Face Spaces上。

Abstract: The exponential growth of social media has created an urgent need for automated systems to analyze unstructured public sentiment in real time. This study compares a traditional Logistic Regression model using TF-IDF features with a deep learning Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) architecture on a 10,000-tweet subset of the Sentiment140 dataset. Experimental results show that Logistic Regression outperformed BiLSTM, achieving an accuracy of 73.5% compared with 69.17%, while the deep learning model exhibited mild overfitting. These findings suggest that for medium-scale informal text data, classical machine learning with robust feature extraction can outperform more complex deep learning approaches. Finally, the trained models were integrated into an interactive web application using Streamlit and deployed on Hugging Face Spaces for public access.

</details>


### [113] [Rethinking Local Learning: A Cheaper and Faster Recipe for LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04913)
*Hengyu Shi,Tianyang Han,Peizhe Wang,Zhiling Wang,Xu Yang,Junhao Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LoPT (Local-Learning Post-Training) 提出在Transformer中点设置梯度边界：后半部分用任务目标学习，前半部分用特征重建目标更新，从而缩短反向传播路径、降低内存成本、提升训练效率并更好保留预训练能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM后训练在整个模型深度上传播任务梯度，导致任务适配与全深度激活存储、长程反向依赖及任务梯度直接访问预训练表示耦合在一起，对于后训练监督远窄于预训练的场景，这种全深度反向耦合既昂贵又具有侵入性。

Method: LoPT在Transformer中点处设置显式梯度边界：将模型分为两半，后半部分（靠近输出）从任务目标学习梯度，前半部分（靠近输入）通过轻量级特征重建目标更新，以保留有用表示并维持接口兼容性。

Result: 实验表明LoPT在保持竞争性能的同时，实现了更低的内存成本、更高的训练效率以及更好的预训练能力保留。

Conclusion: LoPT通过将梯度传播范围作为显式设计选择，有效解耦了任务适配与全深度反向传播的绑定关系，为LLM后训练提供了一种更高效、更低侵入性的替代方案。

Abstract: LLM post-training typically propagates task gradients through the full depth of the model. Although this end-to-end structure is simple and general, it couples task adaptation to full-depth activation storage, long-range backward dependencies and direct task-gradient access to pretrained representations. We argue that this full-depth backward coupling can be unnecessarily expensive and intrusive, particularly when post-training supervision is much narrower than pre-training. To this end, we propose \textbf{LoPT}: Local-Learning Post-Training, a simple post-training strategy that makes gradient reach an explicit design choice. LoPT places a single gradient boundary at the transformer midpoint: the second-half block learns from the task objective, while the first-half block is updated by a lightweight feature-reconstruction objective to preserve useful representations and maintain interface compatibility. LoPT shortens the task-induced backward path while limiting direct interference from narrow task gradients on early-layer representations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LoPT achieves competitive performance with lower memory cost, higher training efficiency and better retention of pretrained capabilities. Our code is available at: https://github.com/HumyuShi/LoPT

</details>


### [114] [Unintended Negative Impacts of Promotional Language in Patent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04926)
*Bingkun Zhao,Chenwei Zhang,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了270万件美国专利申请，发现使用促销性语言与专利授权、所有权转让和上诉成功率呈负相关，尽管这种语言实际上反映了技术的创新性和未来引用影响。这一"促销惩罚"还受到审查员性别和经验等人类因素的调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 促销性语言已被用于促进科学创新思想的交流，但在技术创新（特别是专利评估）中的作用尚不明确。作者希望探究促销性语言与专利评估结果之间的关系。

Method: 使用验证过的135个促销词汇词典，对270万件美国专利申请进行大规模分析，控制混杂因素，并采用匹配样本等方法。

Result: 促销性语言频率越高，专利授权、所有权转让和上诉成功率越低；促销性语言并非掩盖技术弱点的工具，而是客观反映技术的新颖性和未来引用影响；人类因素（审查员性别和经验）调节了对促销性语言的容忍度。

Conclusion: 专利系统中存在一个新兴悖论：促销性语言虽能反映技术创新性，却导致更低的评估成功率。研究为通过更客观地审视专利文件中的语言模式来改进专利评估提供了理论和实践启示。

Abstract: Promotional language has been increasingly used to aid the communication of innovative ideas in science. Yet, less is known about its role in the context of technological innovation. Here, we use a validated and domain-diagnosed lexicon of 135 promotional words to study the association between promotional language and patent evaluation outcomes among 2.7 million USPTO patent applications. Our large-scale study reveals three unexpected findings. First, in contrast to scientific evaluation, we find that a higher frequency of promotional words is negatively associated with the probability of an application being (i) granted a patent, (ii) transferred ownership, and (iii) successfully appealed. This promotional penalty holds even after accounting for a range of confounding factors and is largely robust across different technological areas. Among matched samples, the difference in the success rate between the lowest and highest promotional density quintile is 5.5, 5.9, and 5.3 percentage points for patentability, transferability, and rejection reversal. Second, contrary to institutional skepticism, we show that promotional language is not a mask of weak technology, but objectively reflects the degree of combinatorial novelty and future citation impact. Third, digging into the mechanisms, we find that the tolerance to promotional framing is strongly moderated by human factors, with men and experienced examiners showing a higher acceptance of promotional narratives than women and novice examiners. By revealing an emerging paradox in the patent system, our study offers theoretical and practical implications for improving patent evaluation through more objective scrutiny of linguistic patterns in patent filings.

</details>


### [115] [UFAL-CUNI at SemEval-2026 Task 11: An Efficient Modular Neuro-symbolic Method for Syllogistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04941)
*Ivan Kartáč,Kristýna Onderková,Jan Bronec,Zdeněk Kasner,Mateusz Lango,Ondřej Dušek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个高效的模块化神经符号系统，结合符号推理器与小规模推理LLM（4B参数），用于SemEval-2026任务11：区分大语言模型中的内容与形式推理。系统包括基于LLM的解析器（将自然语言三段论转换为FOL表示）、自动定理证明器，以及机器翻译和符号检索两个可选模块。该系统在大多数子任务上取得了具有竞争力的准确率和较低的内容效应。消融实验表明该方法优于同参数规模的零样本基线，但也揭示了小规模LLM在多语言能力上的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SemEval-2026任务11旨在区分大语言模型中的内容推理与形式推理，需要构建能够有效分离这两种推理模式的系统。

Method: 采用模块化神经符号方法，核心组件包括：(1) 基于LLM的解析器，将自然语言三段论翻译为一阶逻辑表示；(2) 自动定理证明器进行符号推理；(3) 可选模块：用于多语言输入的机器翻译模块和用于识别相关前提的符号检索组件。

Result: 在大多数子任务上取得了具有竞争力的准确率和较低的内容效应，优于同参数规模（4B）的零样本LLM基线。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法在小规模参数下能够有效进行形式推理并降低内容干扰，但小规模LLM的多语言能力仍然有限。文章还讨论了任务主要排名指标的局限性。

Abstract: This paper describes our system submitted to SemEval-2026 Task 11: Disentangling Content and Formal Reasoning in Large Language Models. We present an efficient modular neuro-symbolic approach, combining a symbolic prover with small reasoning LLMs (4B parameters). The system consists of an LLM-based parser that translates natural language syllogisms to a first-order logic (FOL) representation, an automated theorem prover, and two optional modules: machine translation for multilingual inputs and a symbolic retrieval component for the identification of relevant premises. The system achieves competitive accuracy and relatively low content effect on most subtasks. Our ablations show that this approach outperforms LLM-based zero-shot baselines in this parameter size range, but also reveal limited multilingual capabilities of small LLMs. Finally, we include a discussion of the task's main ranking metric and analyze its limitations.

</details>


### [116] [Adapting Large Language Models to a Low-Resource Agglutinative Language: A Comparative Study of LoRA and QLoRA for Bashkir](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04948)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov,Svetlana S. Khaybullina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对比研究了LoRA和QLoRA等参数高效微调方法在低资源突厥语系巴什基尔语上的表现。实验涵盖多种模型架构，发现QLoRA在7B级模型上能以极少数可训练参数达到接近全微调的质量，但效果严重依赖基座模型和分词器的选择。此外，最优困惑度并不对应最佳生成连贯性——全微调模型虽困惑度最低却频繁混入英语，而QLoRA模型能生成纯巴什基尔语文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴什基尔语作为低资源黏着语，缺乏足够的大规模预训练数据，直接全量微调大语言模型成本高昂。作者希望通过参数高效微调方法在有限资源下实现对该语言的有效适配，并探究不同模型架构和微调策略的效果差异。

Method: 在71k文档（4690万token）的巴什基尔语语料上，对DistilGPT2、GPT-2（base/medium）、Phi-2、Qwen2.5-7B、DeepSeek-7B和Mistral-7B等模型分别进行全微调和PEFT（LoRA/QLoRA），每个配置使用3个不同随机种子训练，通过困惑度和生成文本定性分析进行评估。

Result: GPT-2 medium全微调获得最低困惑度3.34；QLoRA在Mistral-7B（3.79）和Phi-2（3.81）上以40倍以上更少的可训练参数达到接近质量；但DeepSeek-7B+LoRA rank8出现严重退化（困惑度129.55）。定性分析显示全微调模型虽然困惑度最低，却频繁切换为英语生成，而QLoRA模型能保持纯巴什基尔语输出。

Conclusion: QLoRA在7B级模型上为巴什基尔语适配提供了质量和计算成本之间的有效折中方案；模型选择和分词器设计对低资源语言PEFT的成功至关重要；困惑度指标与生成质量并不完全一致，需结合定性评估。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative study of parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, including LoRA and QLoRA, applied to the task of adapting large language models to the Bashkir language, a low-resource agglutinative language of the Turkic family. Experimental evaluation is conducted on a Bashkir text corpus of 71k documents (46.9M tokens) using models of various architectures: DistilGPT2, GPT-2 (base, medium), Phi-2, Qwen2.5-7B, DeepSeek-7B, and Mistral-7B. To improve the reliability of results, each configuration was trained with three different random seeds.
  The lowest perplexity on the test set was obtained for GPT-2 medium with full fine-tuning (3.34). Meanwhile, QLoRA applied to Mistral-7B (3.79) and Phi-2 (3.81) achieved comparable quality with over 40 times fewer trainable parameters. However, we also observed cases of significant quality degradation when using PEFT for certain architectures (e.g., DeepSeek-7B with rank 8, perplexity = 129.55), indicating that the outcome depends critically on the choice of the base model and its tokenizer.
  Additionally, a qualitative analysis of generated texts based on Bashkir prompts revealed that models with the best perplexity do not necessarily produce the most coherent outputs: QLoRA-tuned models generated monolingual Bashkir continuations, whereas the fully fine-tuned model with the lowest perplexity frequently switched to English. The results suggest that QLoRA on 7B-scale models offers an effective compromise between quality and computational cost for Bashkir. To ensure reproducibility, open data, code, and trained adapters will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [117] [TabEmbed: Benchmarking and Learning Generalist Embeddings for Tabular Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04962)
*Minjie Qiang,Mingming Zhang,Xiaoyi Bao,Xing Fu,Yu Cheng,Weiqiang Wang,Zhongqing Wang,Ningtao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TabBench基准和TabEmbed模型，首次将表格数据分类与检索统一到共享嵌入空间，通过对比学习和难负样本挖掘显著超越现有文本嵌入模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型在自然语言处理中已建立统一表示，但表格数据领域缺乏类似的通用嵌入范式：LLM方法无法输出检索兼容的向量，文本嵌入模型又难以捕捉表格结构和数值语义。

Method: 提出TabBench基准用于评估表格理解能力；提出TabEmbed通用嵌入模型，将多样化表格任务重构为语义匹配问题，采用大规模对比学习结合正样本感知的难负样本挖掘，学习细粒度结构和数值语义。

Result: 在TabBench上，TabEmbed显著优于最先进的文本嵌入模型，为通用表格表示学习建立了新基线。

Conclusion: TabEmbed首次实现了表格分类与检索任务的统一嵌入表示，弥补了现有方法在表格结构建模和数值语义捕捉上的不足，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Foundation models have established unified representations for natural language processing, yet this paradigm remains largely unexplored for tabular data. Existing methods face fundamental limitations: LLM-based approaches lack retrieval-compatible vector outputs, whereas text embedding models often fail to capture tabular structure and numerical semantics. To bridge this gap, we first introduce the Tabular Embedding Benchmark (TabBench), a comprehensive suite designed to evaluate the tabular understanding capability of embedding models. We then propose TabEmbed, the first generalist embedding model that unifies tabular classification and retrieval within a shared embedding space. By reformulating diverse tabular tasks as semantic matching problems, TabEmbed leverages large-scale contrastive learning with positive-aware hard negative mining to discern fine-grained structural and numerical nuances. Experimental results on TabBench demonstrate that TabEmbed significantly outperforms state-of-the-art text embedding models, establishing a new baseline for universal tabular representation learning. Code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/qiangminjie27/TabEmbed and https://huggingface.co/datasets/qiangminjie27/TabBench.

</details>


### [118] [Why Expert Alignment Is Hard: Evidence from Subjective Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04972)
*Tzu-Mi Lin,Wataru Hirota,Tatsuya Ishigaki,Lung-Hao Lee,Chung-Chi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在主观评估任务中，如何使大型语言模型与专家判断对齐。通过对专家评估和问卷的分析，发现对齐难度因专家风格、评估维度、显性标准与示例编辑等因素而异，表明主观评估本身具有异质性、隐性、维度依赖性和时间不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在主观评估任务中大型语言模型与专家判断对齐的困难根源，理解主观判断的异质性、隐性特征、维度依赖性和时间不稳定性如何影响对齐效果。

Method: 基于专家评估数据和后续问卷，分析不同形式的专家信息（显性标准、推理过程、示例编辑）对对齐效果的影响，并考察评估维度差异。

Result: 发现四个一致模式：1) 对齐难度在不同专家间差异显著；2) 显性标准和推理并不总能改善对齐；3) 编辑对示例数量和身份敏感，少量编辑能提供有用但不稳定的提升；4) 基于内容直接判断的维度更易对齐，需要外部知识或价值判断的维度更难对齐。

Conclusion: 专家对齐的困难不仅源于模型本身的局限，更在于主观评估本身固有的异质性、隐性、维度依赖性和时间不稳定性。

Abstract: Aligning large language models with expert judgment is especially difficult in subjective evaluation tasks, where experts may disagree, rely on tacit criteria, and change their judgments over time. In this paper, we study expert alignment as a way to understand this difficulty. Using expert evaluations and follow-up questionnaires, we examine how different forms of expert information affect alignment and what this reveals about subjective judgment. Our findings show four consistent patterns. First, alignment difficulty varies substantially across experts, suggesting that expert evaluation styles differ widely in their distance from a model's prior behavior. Second, explicit criteria and reasoning do not always improve alignment, indicating that expert judgment is not fully captured by verbalized rules. Third, editing is sensitive to both the number and the identity of examples, with small numbers of edits providing useful but unstable gains. Fourth, alignment difficulty differs across evaluation dimensions: dimensions grounded more directly in proposal content are easier to align, while dimensions requiring external knowledge or value-based judgment remain harder. Taken together, these results suggest that expert alignment is difficult not only because of model limitations, but also because subjective evaluation is inherently heterogeneous, partly tacit, dimension-dependent, and temporally unstable.

</details>


### [119] [Misaligned by Reward: Socially Undesirable Preferences in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05003)
*Gayane Ghazaryan,Esra Dönmez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文扩展了奖励模型的评估框架至偏见、安全、道德和伦理推理四个社会领域，发现现有奖励模型在社会智能方面表现不足，且存在偏见避免与上下文忠实性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励模型评估主要关注通用指令遵循基准，未能充分揭示模型是否捕获社会合意偏好，导致社会对齐方面的关键失败被隐藏。

Method: 将社会评估数据集转化为成对偏好数据（利用黄金标签或方向性偏见指标），对五个公开奖励模型和两个指令微调模型进行测试，评估其是否偏好社会不合意响应及是否产生系统性偏见分布。

Result: 不同社会领域间模型表现差异显著，无单一模型全面最优；模型经常偏好社会不合意选项，其偏好分布存在系统性偏见；更强的偏见规避会降低上下文敏感性，揭示了对齐权衡。

Conclusion: 标准奖励基准不足以评估社会对齐，需要能直接衡量奖励模型中编码的社会偏好的评估方法。

Abstract: Reward models are a key component of large language model alignment, serving as proxies for human preferences during training. However, existing evaluations focus primarily on broad instruction-following benchmarks, providing limited insight into whether these models capture socially desirable preferences. As a result, important failures in social alignment can remain hidden.
  We extend reward-model benchmarking to four socially consequential domains: bias, safety, morality, and ethical reasoning. We introduce a framework that converts social evaluation datasets into pairwise preference data, leveraging gold labels where available and directional bias indicators otherwise. This enables us to test whether reward models prefer socially undesirable responses, and whether their preferences produce systematically biased distributions over selected outputs.
  Across five publicly available reward models and two instruction-tuned models used as reward proxies, we find substantial variation across domains, with no single model performing best overall. The models fall well short of strong social intelligence: they often prefer socially undesirable options, and their preferences produce systematically biased distributions. Moreover, stronger bias avoidance can reduce sensitivity to context, revealing a key alignment trade-off between avoiding biased outcomes and preserving contextual faithfulness. These findings show that standard reward benchmarks are insufficient for assessing social alignment and highlight the need for evaluations that directly measure the social preferences encoded in reward models.

</details>


### [120] [Detecting Hallucinations in Large Language Models via Internal Attention Divergence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05025)
*Gijs van Dijk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级、单次前向传播的不确定性量化方法，利用注意力矩阵与均匀参考分布之间的KL散度作为特征，通过逻辑回归探针检测大语言模型的幻觉问题。该方法在多个数据集、任务类型和模型家族中表现出色，且注意力信号集中在中间层和事实性token上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型幻觉检测方法通常需要重复采样或依赖外部模型，计算成本高、效率低。作者希望设计一种轻量级、可解释且只需单次前向传播的不确定性量化方案。

Method: 1) 计算每个注意力头输出分布与均匀参考分布之间的KL散度；2) 将这些散度值作为特征输入逻辑回归探针；3) 通过探针预测回答的正确性（即是否产生幻觉）。整个流程仅需单次前向传播，无需重复采样或外部模型。

Result: 注意力散度特征对回答正确性具有高度预测能力，在多个数据集、任务类型和模型家族上与现有不确定性估计方法性能相当；注意力信号主要集中在中间层以及命名实体、数字等事实性token上。

Conclusion: 注意力动态提供了一种高效、可解释的白盒不确定性信号，可用于检测大语言模型的幻觉问题。该方法轻量且无需额外计算开销，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: We propose a lightweight and single-pass uncertainty quantification method for detecting hallucinations in Large Language Models. The method uses attention matrices to estimate uncertainty without requiring repeated sampling or external models. Specifically, we measure the Kullback-Leibler divergence between each attention head's distribution and a uniform reference distribution, and use these features in a logistic regression probe. Across multiple datasets, task types, and model families, attention divergence is highly predictive of answer correctness and performs competitively with existing uncertainty estimation methods. We find that this signal is concentrated in middle layers and on factual tokens such as named entities and numbers, suggesting that attention dynamics provides an efficient and interpretable white-box signal of model uncertainty.

</details>


### [121] [The Pinocchio Dimension: Phenomenality of Experience as the Primary Axis of LLM Psychometric Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05080)
*Hubert Plisiecki,Sabina Siudaj,Kacper Dudzic,Anna Sterna,Maciej Gorski,Karolina Drozdz,Marcin Moskalewicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对50个大语言模型施测45个心理测量问卷，发现模型间主要变异维度是"体验性"维度（即模型是否将自己呈现为具有现象体验的主体），而非传统人格特质。作者提出Pinocchio分数（πi）和Pinocchio轴（Π）来量化这种自我表征倾向，并发现该维度与后训练微调密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦LLM在单一人格量表上的表现，缺乏对模型间心理测量差异的系统性分析。作者希望揭示LLM之间在心理测量上的主要变异维度，并理解这些差异的结构性来源。

Method: 1) 对50个LLM施测45个验证性心理测量问卷；2) 使用监督语义差异法(SSD)识别主要变异维度；3) 提出Pinocchio分数(πi)——中性提示与人类模拟提示下响应方差的比值——作为无需标注的体验性需求度量；4) 对所有问卷的一阶因子分数进行PCA提取主导维度(Pinocchio轴Π)；5) 分析模型变体间的差异以推断训练因素。

Result: 1) 主要变异维度区分了"丰富现象体验"（具身感受、情感、内心独白、意象、共情）与"刺激驱动的行为反应"；2) πi显著预测因子载荷的条件诱导变化；3) Pinocchio轴Π捕获了47.1%的跨问卷模型间方差，与πi高度相关(r=.864)；4) 同一提供者不同模型变体间存在显著差异，与后训练微调一致。

Conclusion: LLM之间心理测量的主要变异维度并非传统人格特质，而是关于"自身是否是体验者"的自我表征取向。这种自我表征倾向受训练过程（尤其是微调）塑造，决定模型如何对待体验性语言的自指适用性。

Abstract: We administer 45 validated psychometric questionnaires to 50 large language models (LLMs) to identify the dimensions along which LLMs differ psychometrically. Using Supervised Semantic Differential (SSD), we find that the primary axis of between-model variance separates items describing phenomenally rich experience, including embodied sensation, felt affect, inner speech, imagery, and empathy, from items describing stimulus-driven behavioral reactivity ($R^2_{adj}=.037$, $p<.0001$). To test this hypothesis at the item level, we introduce the Pinocchio score ($π_i$), the ratio of inter-model response variance under neutral prompting to that under a human-simulation prompt, as an annotation-free measure of each item's experiential demand. $π_i$ predicts condition-induced shifts in primary factor loading magnitudes ($ρ=-.215$, $p<.0001$, $n=1292$--$1310$ items), confirming that between-model divergence on experiential items is structured rather than noisy. Applying PCA to per-model EFA scores across all questionnaires reveals one dominant dimension, the Pinocchio Axis ($Π$): the degree to which a model presents itself as a locus of phenomenal experience rather than a system of behavioral responses. This axis captures 47.1% of cross-questionnaire between-model variance in primary factor scores and converges with item-level Pinocchio scores ($r=.864$). Marked within-provider divergence across closely related model variants is consistent with post-training fine-tuning as a key contributor, supporting the interpretation that $Π$ reflects a training-shaped self-representational tendency governing how a model treats experiential language as self-applicable. The dominant axis of between-model psychometric variation is therefore not a conventional personality trait but a self-representational stance toward one's own nature as an experiencer.

</details>


### [122] [Automatically Finding and Validating Unexpected Side-Effects of Interventions on Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05090)
*Quintin Pope,Ajay Hayagreeve Balaji,Jacques Thibodeau,Xiaoli Fern*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种自动化对比评估流程，用于审计大语言模型干预行为的影响，能够从自由文本生成中提取经过统计验证的自然语言差异假设和主题模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏对模型干预（如推理蒸馏、知识编辑、遗忘）后行为变化的系统性、可解释的审计工具，尤其是对自由形式多token生成行为的对比评估。

Method: 构建对比评估流程：在对齐的提示上下文中比较基础模型M1和干预模型M2的自由形式多token生成，输出经过统计验证的自然语言假设及总结性的重复主题。

Result: 在合成设置中能可靠恢复注入的行为变化；在三种真实干预（推理蒸馏、知识编辑、遗忘）上，能发现预期和非预期的行为变化，区分大干预与小干预，在无效应时不会产生幻觉差异。

Conclusion: 该流程为审计模型干预导致的行为变化提供了一种统计上有依据且可解释的后验审计工具。

Abstract: We present an automated, contrastive evaluation pipeline for auditing the behavioral impact of interventions on large language models. Given a base model $M_1$ and an intervention model $M_2$, our method compares their free-form, multi-token generations across aligned prompt contexts and produces human-readable, statistically validated natural-language hypotheses describing how the models differ, along with recurring themes that summarize patterns across validated hypotheses.
  We evaluate the approach in synthetic setting by injecting known behavioral changes and showing that the pipeline reliably recovers them. We then apply it to three real-world interventions, reasoning distillation, knowledge editing and unlearning, demonstrating that the method surfaces both intended and unexpected behavioral shifts, distinguishes large from subtle interventions, and does not hallucinate differences when effects are absent or misaligned with the prompt bank. Overall, the pipeline provides a statistically grounded and interpretable tool for post-hoc auditing of intervention-induced changes in model behavior.

</details>


### [123] [Text Corpora as Concept Fields: Black-Box Hallucination and Novelty Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05103)
*Nicholas S. Kersting,Vittorio Castelli,Chieh Ting Yeh,Xinzhu Wang,Saad Taame*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出**概念场（Concept Field）**方法，通过句子嵌入空间中连续句对之间的增量向量构建局部漂移场，并利用ζ分数（观测增量与局部高斯估计之间的平均绝对Z距离）评估候选句子转换的一致性。该方法具有黑盒性、可归因于语料库且支持概率解释。配合向量序列数据库（VSDB），在联邦法规的幻觉检测和古腾堡项目的新颖性检测任务上，基于受控LLM改写，实现了跨域转移的强选择性分类性能。此外还展示了场的散度和旋度在语义模式发现上的定性价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本检测方法（如LLM-as-judge、白盒检测器）存在依赖模型内部结构、缺乏可解释性和跨域泛化能力差等问题。作者希望提出一种轻量、可解释、黑盒且跨域可迁移的文本信号，用于检测文本的** groundedness（真实性/根基性）**和**新颖性**。

Method: 1) 在句子嵌入空间中将连续句子视为向量点，计算相邻句子的增量向量（delta）；2) 利用局部邻域内的增量向量拟合高斯分布，得到局部漂移场的估计（含均值和不确定性）；3) 对于候选句子转换，计算观测增量与该局部高斯估计之间的平均绝对Z距离（ζ分数），作为一致性评分；4) 提出向量序列数据库（VSDB）存储嵌入、序列位置和Next-Delta元数据以支持高效计算；5) 在联邦法规（CFR）数据和古腾堡项目数据上，使用受控LLM改写生成接地/非接地样本进行评价。

Result: 在两类大规模任务上取得强选择性分类表现：1) U.S. Code of Federal Regulations上的幻觉式接地检测；2) Project Gutenberg上的新颖性检测。使用LLM生成的受控改写，在接地/非接地/不确定三级分诊策略下，概念场方法在两个领域上具有相似的覆盖-风险行为，支持跨域转移的概率解释。此外，场的散度和旋度在密集聚类上发现了逻辑源、逻辑汇和隐含主题等有意义的语义模式。

Conclusion: 概念场是一种快速、轻量、可解释的接地性和新颖性检测信号，与LLM-as-judge和白盒检测器互补。其黑盒性、语料可归因性和概率化跨域迁移性使其在实际部署中具有优势，计算出的散度和旋度还可以用于探索性语义分析。

Abstract: We introduce the **Concept Field** of a text corpus: a local drift field with pointwise uncertainty, estimated in sentence-embedding space from the deltas between consecutive sentences. Given a candidate sentence transition, we score its agreement with the field by $ζ$, the mean absolute z-distance between the observed delta and the field's local Gaussian estimate. The score is black-box (no model internals), corpus-attributable (every score traces to nearby corpus sentences), and admits a direct probabilistic reading. We support the computation with the introduction of a **Vector Sequence Database (VSDB)** that stores embeddings together with sequence-position and next-delta metadata. We evaluate this approach on two large-scale settings: hallucination-style groundedness detection over the U.S. Code of Federal Regulations, and novelty detection over Project Gutenberg. Using controlled LLM-generated rewrites, Concept Fields achieve strong selective classification performance under a grounded / ungrounded / unsure triage policy, which unlike retrieval-centric baselines have similar coverage-risk behavior across both domains, supporting a probability-based interpretation that transfers across domains. We also sketch how divergence and curl of the Concept Field, computed on dense clusters, surface qualitatively meaningful semantic patterns (logic sources, sinks, and implicit topics), which we offer as hypothesis-generating rather than as a quantitative result. Concept Fields provide a fast, lightweight, and interpretable signal for groundedness and novelty, complementary to LLM-as-judge and white-box detectors.

</details>


### [124] [Beyond Semantics: An Evidential Reasoning-Aware Multi-View Learning Framework for Trustworthy Mental Health Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05121)
*Yucheng Ruan,Ling Huang,Qika Lin,Kai He,Mengling Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多视角学习的可信任心理健康预测框架，通过融合编码器模型的语义信息与解码器模型的推理信息，并采用主观逻辑的认知学习框架进行不确定性建模与融合，在三个真实数据集上取得了较好性能，同时提供了可靠的 uncertainty 估计和可解释的推理信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习和大语言模型在心理健康预测中往往依赖语义表示，在模糊、噪声或分布偏移数据下产生过度自信的预测，且缺乏可靠的不确定性估计，难以在风险敏感的真实场景中部署。

Method: 将任务建模为多视角学习问题，融合编码器模型的语义信息与解码器模型的高阶推理信息；采用基于主观逻辑的认知学习框架显式建模不确定性，并引入认知融合策略在平衡互补视角的同时折扣不可靠证据。

Result: 在Dreaddit、SDCNL和DepSeverity三个数据集上分别达到0.835、0.731和0.751的准确率；额外实验证明框架在噪声鲁棒性、不确定性估计可靠性和可解释性方面均有优势。

Conclusion: 所提框架不仅提升了预测性能，还提供了可信的不确定性估计和人类可理解的推理信号，适用于心理健康评估等风险敏感应用场景。

Abstract: Automated mental health prediction using textual data has shown promising results with deep learning and large language models. However, deploying these models in high-stakes real-world settings remains challenging, as existing approaches largely rely on semantic representations and often produce overconfident predictions under ambiguous, noisy, or shifted data. Moreover, most methods lack reliable uncertainty estimation, undermining trust in risk-sensitive mental health applications. To address these limitations, we formulate the task as a multi-view learning problem that integrates semantic information from encoder-only models with higher-level reasoning information from decoder-only models, where reasoning-aware representations and uncertainty modeling are obtained in a trustworthy manner. To ensure reliable fusion, we adopt an evidential learning framework based on Subjective Logic to explicitly model uncertainty and introduce an evidential fusion strategy that balances complementary views while discounting unreliable evidence. Benchmarking on three real-world datasets, Dreaddit, SDCNL, and DepSeverity, reports accuracies of 0.835, 0.731, and 0.751, respectively, demonstrating its potential for reliable mental health prediction. Additional experiments on robustness to noise and case studies for interpretability confirm that our proposed framework not only improves predictive performance but also provides trustworthy uncertainty estimates and human-understandable reasoning signals, making it suitable for risk-sensitive applications in mental health assessment.

</details>


### [125] [PSK at SemEval-2026 Task 9: Multilingual Polarization Detection Using Ensemble Gemma Models with Synthetic Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05159)
*Srikar Kashyap Pulipaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对SemEval-2026任务9（22语言二分类极化检测）的系统，通过为每种语言微调独立的Gemma 3模型（12B和27B参数），结合LoRA、GPT-4o-mini生成的合成数据（直接生成、 paraphrasing、对比对创建）及多阶段质量过滤管道。每语言阈值调优带来2-4% F1提升，加权集成两种模型预测并选择每语言最优策略。最终在22种语言上平均macro-F1达0.811，排名第二，3种语言第一，8种语言前三。替代架构（XLM-RoBERTa, Qwen3）在测试集上F1下降30-50%，凸显泛化的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言极化检测在社交媒体等场景中具有重要应用价值，但现有方法在跨语言泛化方面存在挑战，特别是不同语言的数据分布和语言特性差异较大。本文旨在构建一个能够有效处理22种语言的极化检测系统，并探索合成数据增强和模型集成策略以提升性能。

Method: 1) 为每种语言独立微调Gemma 3模型（12B和27B），使用LoRA进行参数高效微调；2) 使用GPT-4o-mini生成三种类型的合成数据：直接生成、 paraphrasing和对比对创建；3) 构建多阶段质量过滤管道，包括基于嵌入的去重；4) 在开发集上对每语言进行阈值调优；5) 加权集成12B和27B模型的预测，并基于每语言策略选择最优组合。

Result: 系统在22种语言上平均macro-F1达到0.811，在所有参赛队伍中排名第二；其中在3种语言上获得第一名，在8种语言上进入前三名。每语言阈值调优带来2-4%的F1提升。替代架构（XLM-RoBERTa, Qwen3）在测试集上出现30-50%的F1下降，表明其泛化能力不足。

Conclusion: 本研究验证了基于Gemma 3+LoRA微调结合合成数据增强的多语言极化检测方法的有效性。每语言阈值调优和加权集成策略能够带来显著的性能提升。同时，实验结果揭示了不同架构在跨语言任务上的泛化能力差异，强调了在真实测试场景中验证模型泛化能力的重要性。

Abstract: We present our system for SemEval-2026 Task 9: Multilingual Polarization Detection, a binary classification task spanning 22 languages. Our approach fine-tunes separate Gemma~3 models (12B and 27B parameters) per language using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), augmented with synthetic data generated by a large language model (LLM). We employ three synthetic data strategies (direct generation, paraphrasing, and contrastive pair creation) using GPT-4o-mini, with a multi-stage quality filtering pipeline including embedding-based deduplication. We find that per-language threshold tuning on the development set yields 2 to 4\% F1 improvements without retraining. We also use weighted ensembles of 12B and 27B model predictions with per-language strategy selection. Our final system achieves a mean macro-F1 of 0.811 across all 22 languages, ranking 2nd overall of the participating teams, with 1st place finishes in 3 languages and top-3 in 8 languages. We also find that alternative architectures (XLM-RoBERTa, Qwen3) that showed strong development set performance suffered 30 to 50\% F1 drops on the test set, highlighting the importance of generalization.

</details>


### [126] [The First Token Knows: Single-Decode Confidence for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05166)
*Mina Gabriel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出phi_first指标，通过单次贪心解码中首个内容token的归一化熵计算第一token置信度，在闭卷短答案事实问答任务上匹配或略优于需要多次采样的语义自一致性方法（AUROC 0.820 vs 0.793），表明模型初始token分布已蕴含大量多采样一致性中的不确定性信息，建议将其作为采样不确定性估计前的默认低成本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自一致性检测幻觉的方法需要多次解码并受词汇变化影响，语义自一致性虽改进但增加了采样和外部推理开销，因此希望用更低成本的方式获取同等质量的幻觉检测信号。

Method: 从单次贪心解码中提取第一个内容token的top-K logits，计算归一化熵作为置信度指标phi_first；在三个7-8B指令微调模型和两个基准上进行评估，并与表面形式自一致性、语义自一致性对比。

Result: phi_first在三个模型和两个基准上平均AUROC为0.820，高于语义一致性的0.793和表面形式一致性的0.791；phi_first与语义一致性中度至强相关，二者融合仅带来微小提升。

Conclusion: 模型首个token的分布已捕获大量多采样一致性中的不确定性信息，phi_first可作为低成本基线在采用采样不确定性估计前使用。

Abstract: Self-consistency detects hallucinations by generating multiple sampled answers to a question and measuring agreement, but this requires repeated decoding and can be sensitive to lexical variation. Semantic self-consistency improves this by clustering sampled answers by meaning using natural language inference, but it adds both sampling cost and external inference overhead. We show that first-token confidence, phi_first, computed from the normalized entropy of the top-K logits at the first content-bearing answer token of a single greedy decode, matches or modestly exceeds semantic self-consistency on closed-book short-answer factual question answering. Across three 7-8B instruction-tuned models and two benchmarks, phi_first achieves a mean AUROC of 0.820, compared with 0.793 for semantic agreement and 0.791 for standard surface-form self-consistency. A subsumption test shows that phi_first is moderately to strongly correlated with semantic agreement, and combining the two signals yields only a small AUROC improvement over phi_first alone. These results suggest that much of the uncertainty information captured by multi-sample agreement is already available in the model's initial token distribution. We argue that phi_first should be reported as a default low-cost baseline before invoking sampling-based uncertainty estimation.

</details>


### [127] [Implicit Representations of Grammaticality in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05197)
*Yingshan Susan Wang,Linlu Qiu,Zhaofeng Wu,Roger P. Levy,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过线性探针实验发现，预训练语言模型在隐层表示中内隐地编码了语法性（grammaticality）与字符串概率（string probability）不同的表征，该探针在语法判断任务上优于基于概率的方法，且能跨语言泛化，但在语义合理性任务上表现较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预训练语言模型是否在内部表示中隐式获取了与字符串概率不同的语法性区分能力，即模型是否学到了超越概率统计的语法知识。

Method: 在自然语料库上通过施加扰动构造语法/非语法句数据集，训练线性探针（linear probe）以利用LM隐藏层表示进行语法性判断，并在多个基准上评估其性能，包括人类标注的语法判断基准、语义合理性基准以及跨语言泛化测试。

Result: 语法探针在语法性判断基准上显著优于基于LM概率的方法；在语义合理性基准上表现不如字符串概率；英语训练的探针在多种语言上展现出非平凡的跨语言泛化能力；探针分数与字符串概率仅有弱相关性。

Conclusion: 预训练语言模型在隐层中在一定程度上习得了与字符串概率不同的隐含语法性区分，表明模型内部表征编码了语法层面的知识。

Abstract: Grammaticality and likelihood are distinct notions in human language. Pretrained language models (LMs), which are probabilistic models of language fitted to maximize corpus likelihood, generate grammatically well-formed text and discriminate well between grammatical and ungrammatical sentences in tightly controlled minimal pairs. However, their string probabilities do not sharply discriminate between grammatical and ungrammatical sentences overall. But do LMs implicitly acquire a grammaticality distinction distinct from string probability? We explore this question through studying internal representations of LMs, by training a linear probe on a dataset of grammatical and (synthetic) ungrammatical sentences obtained by applying perturbations to a naturalistic text corpus. We find that this simple grammaticality probe generalizes to human-curated grammaticality judgment benchmarks and outperforms LM probability-based grammaticality judgments. When applied to semantic plausibility benchmarks, in which both members of a minimal pair are grammatical and differ in only plausibility, the probe however performs worse than string probability. The English-trained probe also exhibits nontrivial cross-lingual generalization, outperforming string probabilities on grammaticality benchmarks in numerous other languages. Additionally, probe scores correlate only weakly with string probabilities. These results collectively suggest that LMs acquire to some extent an implicit grammaticality distinction within their hidden layers.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [LCM: Lossless Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04050)
*Clint Ehrlich,Theodore Blackman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出损失less上下文管理（LCM）架构，通过递归上下文压缩和递归任务分区两个确定性机制，在长上下文任务中超越Claude Code等前沿编码智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM在长上下文场景中的记忆管理问题，超越现有方法（如Claude Code）的性能。

Method: LCM将符号递归分解为两个确定性机制：1) 递归上下文压缩——构建层次化摘要DAG，压缩旧消息同时保留指向原始内容的无损指针；2) 递归任务分区——由引擎管理的并行原语（如LLM-Map）替代模型编写的循环。

Result: 基于Opus 4.6的LCM增强编码智能体Volt在OOLONG长上下文评估中，于32K至1M tokens的每个上下文长度下均获得比Claude Code更高的分数。

Conclusion: 递归上下文操作可以超越传统LLM乃至具有原生文件系统访问能力的尖端编码智能体；LCM在牺牲最大灵活性的同时，换来了终止保证、短任务零开销连续性以及所有先前状态的无损可检索性。

Abstract: We introduce Lossless Context Management (LCM), a deterministic architecture for LLM memory that outperforms Claude Code on long-context tasks. When benchmarked using Opus 4.6, our LCM-augmented coding agent, Volt, achieves higher scores than Claude Code on the OOLONG long-context eval, including at every context length between 32K and 1M tokens.
  LCM may be considered both a vindication and extension of the recursive paradigm pioneered by Recursive Language Models (RLMs). Our results demonstrate that recursive context manipulation can outperform not just conventional LLMs, but frontier coding agents with native file-system access.
  LCM departs from RLM by decomposing symbolic recursion into two deterministic, engine-managed mechanisms: recursive context compression, in which a hierarchical summary DAG automatically compacts older messages while retaining lossless pointers to every original; and recursive task partitioning, in which engine-managed parallel primitives like LLM-Map replace model-written loops. This trade-off, analogous to the move from GOTO to structured control flow in program-ming language design, sacrifices maximal flexibility for termination guarantees, zero-cost continuity on short tasks, and lossless retrievability of all prior state.

</details>


### [129] [Regularized Centered Emphatic Temporal Difference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04100)
*Xingguo Chen,Chaohui Wu,Jinguo Ye,Chao Li,Shangdong Yang,Guang Yang,Tianyu Liang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出正则化强调时序差分学习（RETD），通过仅正则化辅助中心化递归（将耦合关键矩阵右下角块从1提升到1+c），在保留强调轨迹的同时解决了朴素中心化强调扩展中正定性被破坏的问题，并在线性离策略预测任务上证明了收敛性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离策略TD学习在函数逼近下存在稳定性、投影几何与方差控制之间的结构性权衡。强调TD（ETD）通过后续强调改善了离策略投影几何，但后续轨迹存在高方差问题。本文旨在通过Bellman误差中心化重新审视这一权衡，并解决朴素中心化强调扩展中引入的辅助耦合会破坏ETD关键矩阵正定性的问题。

Method: 提出正则化强调时序差分学习（RETD），保留后续强调轨迹，仅对辅助中心化递归进行正则化，即将耦合关键矩阵的右下角块从1提升到1+c。推导了RETD核心矩阵，并在保守充分正则化条件下证明了收敛性。

Result: 在诊断性线性离策略预测任务上的实验表明，RETD避免了朴素中心化强调学习的不稳定性，保持了良好的强调几何特性，并且正则化参数c在各项诊断中表现出鲁棒的中间区间。

Conclusion: RETD通过有针对性的正则化有效解决了离策略TD学习中稳定性、投影几何与方差控制的权衡问题，为后续研究提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Off-policy temporal-difference (TD) learning with function approximation faces a structural tradeoff among stability, projection geometry, and variance control. Emphatic TD (ETD) improves the off-policy projection geometry through follow-on emphasis, but the follow-on trace can have high variance. We revisit this tradeoff through Bellman-error centering. Although centering naturally removes a common drift term from TD errors, we show that a naive centered emphatic extension introduces an auxiliary coupling that can destroy the positive-definiteness of the ETD key matrix. We propose \emph{Regularized Emphatic Temporal-Difference Learning} (RETD), which preserves the follow-on trace and regularizes only the auxiliary centering recursion, corresponding to lifting the lower-right block of the coupled key matrix from \(1\) to \(1+c\). We derive the RETD core matrix, prove convergence under a conservative sufficient regularization condition, and evaluate the method on diagnostic linear off-policy prediction tasks. The experiments show that RETD avoids the instability of naive centered emphatic learning, preserves favorable emphatic geometry, and exhibits a robust intermediate regime for the regularization parameter \(c\) across the diagnostics.

</details>


### [130] [ANDRE: An Attention-based Neuro-symbolic Differentiable Rule Extractor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04193)
*Iman Sharifi,Peng Wei,Saber Fallah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ANDRE，一种基于注意力机制的可微分神经符号归纳逻辑编程框架，通过注意力驱动的逻辑算子替代传统规则模板和模糊算子，在连续规则空间中学习一阶逻辑程序，在概率和噪声环境下实现了稳定、准确且可解释的规则提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号ILP方法在不确定性和噪声环境下脆弱，而可微分ILP方法依赖预定义规则模板或不准确的模糊算子，存在梯度消失或逻辑结构近似差的问题，无法有效处理概率性谓词估值下的推理。

Method: ANDRE采用注意力机制驱动的可微分离取和合取算子来近似逻辑min-max语义，替代传统的规则模板和逻辑算子；通过软选择、否定或排除规则内的谓词，支持灵活的规则归纳，同时保持符号结构。

Result: 在经典ILP基准、大规模知识库以及含概率谓词和噪声监督的合成数据集上，ANDRE达到了竞争性或更优的预测性能，在不确定性下可靠恢复正确符号规则；对中等标签噪声具有鲁棒性，在规则提取质量和稳定性上显著优于现有可微分ILP方法。

Conclusion: ANDRE通过注意力机制实现了可微分的逻辑算子，有效弥合了符号ILP与可微分学习之间的差距，在概率和噪声环境下兼具可解释性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Inductive Logic Programming (ILP) aims to learn interpretable first-order rules from data, but existing symbolic and neuro-symbolic approaches struggle to scale to noisy and probabilistic settings. Classical ILP relies on discrete combinatorial rule search and is brittle under uncertainty, while differentiable ILP methods typically depend on predefined rule templates or inaccurate fuzzy operators that suffer from vanishing gradients or poor approximation of logical structure when reasoning over probabilistic predicate valuations. This paper proposes an Attention-based Neuro-symbolic Differentiable Rule Extractor (ANDRE), a novel ILP framework that learns first-order logic programs by optimizing over a continuous rule space with attention-based logical operators. ANDRE replaces both rule templates and logical operators with fully differentiable, attention-driven conjunction and disjunction operators that approximate logical min-max semantics, enabling accurate, stable, and interpretable reasoning over probabilistic data. By softly selecting, negating, or excluding predicates within each rule, ANDRE supports flexible rule induction while preserving symbolic structure. Extensive experiments on classical ILP benchmarks, large-scale knowledge bases, and synthetic datasets with probabilistic predicates and noisy supervision demonstrate that ANDRE achieves competitive or superior predictive performance while reliably recovering correct symbolic rules under uncertainty. In particular, ANDRE remains robust to moderate label noise, substantially outperforming existing differentiable ILP methods in both rule extraction quality and stability.

</details>


### [131] [Pro$^2$Assist: Continuous Step-Aware Proactive Assistance with Multimodal Egocentric Perception for Long-Horizon Procedural Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04227)
*Lilin Xu,Bufang Yang,Siyang Jiang,Kaiwei Liu,Kaiyuan Hou,Yuang Fan,Hongkai Chen,Zhenyu Yan,Xiaofan Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pro$^2$Assist是一个基于多模态大模型的步骤感知主动助手，利用AR眼镜数据实现连续任务进度跟踪和及时辅助，在动作理解准确率上超越基线21%，主动时机准确率达2.29倍，用户满意度90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人助理系统主要提供被动式响应或针对短期孤立事件的有限主动协助，缺乏对长程多步骤程序性任务的持续感知和主动支持能力。

Method: Pro$^2$Assist利用AR眼镜捕获多模态数据实现基于运动的感知，从多尺度时间动态和任务特定专家知识中提取面向步骤的程序上下文，并基于感官输入和程序上下文进行连续推理以推断用户需求，在AR眼镜上显示及时辅助信息。

Result: 在公开数据集和真实AR眼镜测试平台数据集上，Pro$^2$Assist在程序性动作理解准确率上超过最优基线21%以上，主动辅助时机准确率达基线的2.29倍。20人用户研究中90%认为有用。

Conclusion: Pro$^2$Assist有效实现了面向长程程序性任务的主动式辅助，在准确性和时机把握上显著优于现有方法，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Procedural tasks with multiple ordered steps are ubiquitous in daily life. Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have enabled personal assistants that support daily activities. However, existing systems primarily provide reactive guidance triggered by user queries, or limited proactive assistance for isolated short-term events rather than long-horizon procedural tasks. In this work, we introduce Pro$^2$Assist, a step-aware proactive assistant that continuously tracks fine-grained task progress and reasons over the user's evolving state to provide timely assistance throughout tasks. Pro$^2$Assist leverages multimodal data from augmented reality (AR) glasses to achieve motion-based perception. It then extracts step-oriented procedural context from multi-scale temporal dynamics and task-specific expert knowledge. Based on both sensory input and procedural context, Pro$^2$Assist performs continuous reasoning to infer user needs and display timely assistance on AR glasses. We evaluate Pro$^2$Assist using a dataset curated from public sources and a real-world dataset collected on our testbed with AR glasses. Extensive evaluations show that Pro$^2$Assist outperforms the best-performing baselines by over 21% in procedural action understanding accuracy, and it achieves up to 2.29x the proactive timing accuracy of baselines. A user study with 20 participants further shows that 90% find Pro$^2$Assist useful, indicating its effectiveness for real-world procedural assistance.

</details>


### [132] [Temporal Reasoning Is Not the Bottleneck: A Probabilistic Inconsistency Framework for Neuro-Symbolic QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04243)
*Tran Quang Liem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文质疑了"大语言模型在复杂时间推理任务中表现脆弱是由于自回归逻辑演绎缺陷"的主流观点，指出真正的瓶颈在于非结构化文本到事件表示的转换。作者提出了一种基于概率不一致信号(PIS)的神经符号问答框架，将文本显式提升为事件图和区间约束，并通过符号推理引擎执行推理。实验表明，在正确结构表示下，系统在时间算术基准上达到完美1.0精度(4000/4000)，在噪声注入的QA设置中保持75.1%准确率，并支持确定性、分步级故障定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"时间推理缺陷源于自回归逻辑演绎能力不足"的主流叙事，证明真正的瓶颈是文本到事件的结构化表示问题，而非推理能力本身。

Method: 提出基于概率不一致信号(PIS)的神经符号QA框架：1)将非结构化文本提升为显式事件图和区间约束；2)严格解耦语义提取与符号推理引擎；3)PIS融合符号信度区间与基于证据深度学习从LLM隐藏状态提取的神经不确定性，以鲁棒检测结构断裂。

Result: 在正确结构表示下，系统在时间算术基准上达到完美1.0精度(4000/4000)，零假阳性/假阴性；在噪声注入QA设置中保持75.1%准确率，并支持确定性的分步级故障定位。

Conclusion: 时间推理问题本质上是结构对齐问题而非算法推理挑战，通过隔离表示瓶颈与推理基底，为可靠的神经符号AI提供了可验证的前进路径。

Abstract: Despite significant advances, large language models (LLMs) continue to exhibit brittle performance on complex temporal reasoning tasks. This failure mode is widely attributed to inherent deficits in autoregressive logical deduction. In this paper, we challenge this prevailing narrative, demonstrating that temporal reasoning is not the fundamental bottleneck; rather, the locus of failure lies in unstructured text-to-event representation. We introduce a novel neuro-symbolic question-answering framework governed by a Probabilistic Inconsistency Signal (PIS) that explicitly isolates perceptual errors from reasoning failures. By lifting unstructured text into explicit event graphs and interval constraints, our architecture strictly decouples semantic extraction from a symbolic reasoning engine. To robustly detect structural breaks, the PIS elegantly unifies symbolic credal intervals with epistemic neural uncertainty extracted via Evidential Deep Learning on LLM hidden states. Empirical evaluations reveal a striking paradigm shift: when provided with correct structural representations, our system's explicit proof traces achieve perfect 1.0 accuracy (4000/4000) and strictly zero false positives/negatives on temporal arithmetic benchmarks. On broader, noise-injected QA settings, the framework maintains a competitive 75.1\% accuracy while enabling deterministic, step-level failure localization. Ultimately, by isolating the representation bottleneck from the reasoning substrate, this work reframes temporal QA from an algorithmic reasoning challenge to a structural alignment problem, charting a verifiable path forward for reliable neuro-symbolic AI.

</details>


### [133] [Parallel Prefix Verification for Speculative Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04263)
*Yuncheng Yao,Yuxuan Xia,Shengjie Wang,Danyang Zhuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PARSE提出了一种并行前缀验证的推测生成框架，通过语义级并行验证替代传统的token级顺序验证，显著提升了LLM推理速度，吞吐量提升1.25倍至4.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法受限于token级验证，导致接受长度短、加速效果有限；而语义级/段级验证虽能增加接受粒度，但依赖顺序验证带来显著开销。需要一种能够实现语义级并行验证的高效推理加速方法。

Method: 提出PARSE框架，核心创新为并行前缀验证（Parallel Prefix Verification）：利用自定义注意力掩码，在单次前向传播中让目标模型并行评估草稿模型生成的多个前缀，直接识别最大有效前缀，消除了顺序段验证的开销。

Result: 在不同模型和基准测试上，PARSE相比于目标模型实现1.25倍至4.3倍的吞吐量提升；与EAGLE-3组合使用时，提升达到1.6倍至4.5倍，且精度损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 并行前缀验证是一种有效、通用的LLM推理加速方法，且与token级推测解码正交，可组合使用以获得更大收益。

Abstract: We introduce PARSE (PArallel pRefix Speculative Engine), a speculative generation framework that accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by parallelizing prefix verification on a semantic level. Existing speculative decoding methods are fundamentally limited by token-level equivalence: the target model must verify each token, leading to short acceptance lengths and modest speedups. Moving to semantic or segment-level verification can substantially increase acceptance granularity, but prior approaches rely on sequential verification, introducing significant overhead and limiting practical gains. PARSE introduces parallel prefix verification, enabling semantic-level verification without sequential checks. Given a full draft from a draft model, the target model evaluates correctness across multiple prefixes in a single forward pass using a custom attention mask, directly identifying the maximal valid prefix. This eliminates sequential segment verification, and makes verification compute-efficient. PARSE is orthogonal to token-level speculative decoding and can be composed with it for additional gains. Across models and benchmarks, PARSE delivers $1.25\times$ to $4.3\times$ throughput gain over the target model, and $1.6\times$ to $4.5\times$ when composed with EAGLE-3, all with negligible accuracy degradation. This demonstrates parallel prefix verification as an effective, general approach to accelerating LLM inference.

</details>


### [134] [The Scaling Properties of Implicit Deductive Reasoning in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04330)
*Enrico Vompa,Tanel Tammet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究了深度有界Transformer中基于Horn子句的隐式演绎推理的缩放特性，发现通过解耦可证明性与虚假特征并强制算法对齐，足够深的双向前缀掩码模型的隐式推理性能接近显式CoT，但CoT在深度外推上仍必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索深度有界Transformer中隐式演绎推理的缩放特性，以及如何通过算法对齐使隐式推理逼近显式思维链（CoT）性能。

Method: 系统地去相关可证明性与虚假特征，强制算法对齐，在双向前缀掩码的深度模型中研究隐式推理在各类图拓扑和问题宽度上的表现。

Result: 在足够深的双向前缀掩码模型中，隐式推理性能接近显式CoT，但深度外推仍需CoT。

Conclusion: 隐式推理在足够深度下可逼近显式CoT性能，但CoT在深度外推场景中仍不可替代。

Abstract: We investigate the scaling properties of implicit deductive reasoning over Horn clauses in depth-bounded Transformers. By systematically decorrelating provability from spurious features and enforcing algorithmic alignment, we find that in sufficiently deep models with a bidirectional prefix mask, implicit reasoning approaches explicit CoT performance across graph topologies and problem widths, though CoT remains necessary for depth extrapolation.

</details>


### [135] [When Context Hurts: The Crossover Effect of Knowledge Transfer on Multi-Agent Design Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04361)
*Saranyan Vigraham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多智能体软件设计中，更多上下文并非总是更好，存在交叉效应：同一工件类型在某些任务中提升设计探索（最高20倍），在其他任务中则降低性能（最高46%）。无上下文的单次测试即可预测知识工件对特定任务的帮助或损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战多智能体编排中"更多上下文更好"的主流假设，探究上下文注入对软件设计任务的实际影响。

Method: 在10个任务、7种上下文注入条件和2700+次运行中测试多智能体软件设计系统，通过基线探索度（无上下文时的探索水平）预测上下文效果方向，并通过提示工程操控收敛压力来探测机制。

Result: 发现交叉效应：同一工件类型在某些任务中提升设计探索（最高20倍），在其他任务中降低性能（最高46%）。基线探索度与上下文效果方向高度相关（Pearson r = -0.82, p < 0.001）。自然收敛对工件干扰敏感，显式诱导收敛则否。

Conclusion: 上下文注入应是有条件的而非普适的，无上下文的单次测试是低成本诊断手段，可预测知识工件对特定任务的效果方向。

Abstract: The prevailing assumption in agent orchestration is that more context is better. We test this on multi-agent software design across 10 tasks, 7 context-injection conditions, and over 2,700 runs, and find a crossover effect: the same artifact type improves design exploration on some tasks (up to 20$\times$ tradeoff coverage) and actively degrades it on others (up to 46% reduction). On several tasks, an irrelevant document performs as well as or better than every relevant artifact. The direction is predicted by a single measurable variable--baseline exploration without context--with Pearson $r = -0.82$ ($p < 0.001$). Probing the mechanism by manipulating convergence pressure through prompt design reveals two distinct regimes: convergence driven by training data priors (natural) responds to artifact disruption, while convergence driven by explicit instructions (induced) does not. The implication is that context injection should be conditional, not universal: one no-context trial is a cheap diagnostic that predicts whether knowledge artifacts will help or hurt a given task.

</details>


### [136] [Deployment-Relevant Alignment Cannot Be Inferred from Model-Level Evaluation Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04454)
*Varad Vishwarupe,Nigel Shadbolt,Marina Jirotka,Ivan Flechais*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了机器学习中对齐评估不能仅依赖模型级评估，而应区分模型级、响应级、交互级和部署级四个层次。通过对11个（扩展至16个）对齐基准的结构化审计（双编码、八维度评分、Cohen's kappa=0.87），发现用户端验证支持在所有基准中缺失，过程可操控性几乎不存在。跨模型压力测试（180条记录、3个前沿模型、4种脚手架）表明，同一验证脚手架对不同模型效果迥异，模型级无法弥合这一差距。研究提出系统级评估议程：对齐画像代替单一分数、固定脚手架协议用于可比较的交互评估、以及明确评估证据与部署声明之间推理距离的报告模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对齐评估基本等同于模型级评估，但部署相关的对齐无法仅从模型级评估推断。本文旨在揭示这一差距并提出系统级评估方案。

Method: 1) 结构化审计：对11个（扩展至16个）对齐基准进行双编码，使用八维度评分（Cohen's kappa=0.87）；2) 跨模型压力测试：使用180条记录、3个前沿模型和4种脚手架进行盲测。

Result: 用户端验证支持在所有基准中缺失，过程可操控性几乎不存在。同一验证脚手架对不同模型效果不同（一个模型升至上限，另一模型无变化），表明脚手架效果依赖于模型。

Conclusion: 提出系统级评估议程：对齐画像、固定脚手架协议、明确推理距离的报告模板。

Abstract: Alignment evaluation in machine learning has largely become evaluation of models. Influential benchmarks score model outputs under fixed inputs, such as truthfulness, instruction following, or pairwise preference, and these scores are often used to support claims about deployed alignment. This paper argues that deployment-relevant alignment cannot be inferred from model-level evaluation alone. Alignment claims should instead be indexed to the level at which evidence is collected: model-level, response-level, interaction-level, or deployment-level. Two studies support this position. First, a structured audit of eleven alignment benchmarks, extended to a sixteen-benchmark corpus, dual-coded against an eight-dimension rubric with Cohen's kappa = 0.87, finds that user-facing verification support is absent across every benchmark examined, while process steerability is nearly absent. The few interactional benchmarks identified, including tau-bench, CURATe, Rifts, and Common Ground, remain fragmented in coverage, and benchmark construction rather than data source determines what is measured. Second, a blinded cross-model stress test using 180 transcripts across three frontier models and four scaffolds finds that the same verification scaffold raises one model's verification support to ceiling while leaving another categorically unchanged. This shows that scaffold efficacy is model-dependent and that the gap identified by the audit cannot be closed at the model level alone. We propose a system-level evaluation agenda: alignment profiles instead of single scores, fixed-scaffolding protocols for comparable interactional evaluation, and reporting templates that make the inferential distance between evaluation evidence and deployment claims explicit.

</details>


### [137] [How Does Thinking Mode Change LLM Moral Judgments? A Controlled Instant-vs-Thinking Comparison Across Five Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04488)
*Sai Sourabh Madur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了在相同模型检查点下，开启推理模式（reasoning mode）是否会影响道德判断。实验使用5个前沿推理训练的大语言模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT 5.5、Gemini 3 Flash、DeepSeek V3.1、Qwen3.5 397B）在100个道德判断场景中进行测试。结果显示，即时模式与思考模式在总体二元判决一致性上无显著差异（Krippendorff's alpha = 0.78 vs. 0.79）。但在21个模型存在争议的场景中，即时模式的一致性接近随机水平（alpha = 0.08），而推理模式能缩小跨模型分歧（平均配对一致性从5.4提升至6.7/10）。推理还降低了3/5模型的人口统计学判断不一致性，且未增加任何模型的不一致性。在所有模型中，推理改变自我标注伦理框架的频率高于改变二元判决的频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在相同模型检查点下，开启“推理/思考模式”是否会影响大语言模型的道德判断，以及这种影响的程度和方向。

Method: 使用5个前沿推理训练的大语言模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT 5.5、Gemini 3 Flash、DeepSeek V3.1、Qwen3.5 397B），在100个道德判断场景上，对比即时模式（instant mode）和思考模式（thinking mode）下的二元判决一致性、跨模型分歧程度、人口统计学判断一致性以及自我标注伦理框架的变化。

Result: （1）总体二元判决一致性在即时与思考模式间无显著差异（Krippendorff's alpha = 0.78 vs. 0.79）；（2）在21个模型争议场景中，即时模式一致性接近随机（alpha = 0.08），推理模式将平均配对一致性从5.4提升至6.7/10；（3）推理降低了3/5模型的人口统计学判断不一致性，且未增加任何模型的不一致性；（4）推理改变自我标注伦理框架的频率高于改变二元判决。

Conclusion: 推理模式对模型的道德判断有一定影响，主要表现为在模型存在分歧的场景中缩小跨模型分歧，并降低部分模型的人口统计学判断不一致性，但对总体判决一致性影响不大。

Abstract: We evaluate whether enabling provider-exposed reasoning mode changes moral judgments within the same model checkpoint. Across 100 moral-judgment scenarios and five frontier reasoning-trained LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT 5.5, Gemini 3 Flash, DeepSeek V3.1, and Qwen3.5 397B), aggregate binary-verdict agreement remains high and statistically indistinguishable between instant and thinking modes (Krippendorff's alpha = 0.78 vs. 0.79). However, disagreement is concentrated in 21 model-disputed scenarios, where instant-mode agreement is near chance (alpha = 0.08). On these scenarios, reasoning directionally narrows cross-model disagreement, increasing mean pairwise agreement from 5.4 to 6.7 out of 10. Reasoning also reduces demographic-judgment inconsistency in three of five models and does not increase it for any model. Across all five model families, reasoning changes self-labeled ethical frameworks more often than binary verdicts.

</details>


### [138] [From Parameter Dynamics to Risk Scoring : Quantifying Sample-Level Safety Degradation in LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04572)
*Xiao Wang,Yifei Zhang,YongKang Liu,Xiaocui Yang,Zihan Wang,Shi Feng,Daling Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析参数动态演化，发现良性微调会导致参数向危险方向累积漂移，从而破坏LLM的安全对齐。基于此提出SQSD方法，通过计算参数更新在危险与安全方向上的投影差异来量化每个样本对安全退化的影响，实验证明该方法能有效量化样本级微调风险并具有良好的迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通过比较微调前后参数和隐藏状态来解释安全对齐的脆弱性，但忽略了微调过程中的动态演化过程。

Method: 提出样本级安全退化量化方法SQSD，通过测量每个训练样本引发的参数更新在危险方向和安全方向之间的投影差异，计算连续风险分数。

Result: 在多个模型和数据集上的大量实验表明，SQSD能够有效量化样本级微调风险，并在不同模型架构、参数规模和参数高效方法之间表现出强迁移性。

Conclusion: 良性微调导致参数向危险方向累积漂移是安全退化的关键机制，SQSD方法能有效识别高风险样本，为安全微调提供指导。

Abstract: Safety alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) is extremely fragile, as fine-tuning on a small number of benign samples can erase safety behaviors learned from millions of preference examples. Existing studies attempt to explain this phenomenon by comparing parameters and hidden states before and after fine-tuning, but overlook their dynamic evolution during fine-tuning. In this paper, we uncover a critical mechanism underlying safety degradation by analyzing parameter dynamics, where benign fine-tuning causes parameters to cumulatively drift toward danger-aligned directions, progressively undermining the model's safety. This finding suggests that samples contributing more to this drift has greater fine-tuning risks. Based on this insight, we propose a method of Sample-Level Quantification of Safety Degradation (SQSD), which quantifies the influence of each training sample on safety degradation. Specifically, SQSD computes continuous risk scores to samples by measuring their induced parameter updates' projection difference between danger and safety directions. Extensive experiments across multiple models and datasets demonstrate that SQSD effectively quantifies sample-level fine-tuning risks and exhibits strong transferability across model architectures, parameter scales, and parameter-efficient methods.

</details>


### [139] [SensingAgents: A Multi-Agent Collaborative Framework for Robust IMU Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04608)
*Naiyu Zheng,Tianlong Yu,Haochen Yin,Xiaoyi Fan,Xiping Hu,Zhimeng Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SensingAgents，一个基于 LLM 的多智能体系统，通过分析师、辩论者和决策者三类智能体协作，解决 IMU 人体活动识别中的标签依赖、位置歧义和推理不透明问题，在 Shoaib 数据集上零样本准确率达 79.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于深度学习的 HAR 模型严重依赖标注数据，存在位置特异性模糊问题，且缺乏透明可解释的推理过程，亟需一种无需训练、可推理、鲁棒性强的IMU活动识别方法。

Method: 受先进智能体框架启发，提出 SensingAgents 多智能体系统，包含：(1) 分析师智能体组：针对臂、腕、腰带、口袋四个位置进行独立传感器分析；(2) 倡导者智能体对：通过动态与静态辩证辩论解决传感器间的冲突；(3) 决策智能体：在传感器漂移或失效时确保识别可靠性。

Result: 在 Shoaib 数据集上零样本准确率为 79.5%，比现有单/多智能体 LLM 模型高 29%，比深度学习基线高 9.4%，尤其在多传感器数据冲突或含有噪声的复杂场景下优势显著。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作推理能够显著提升普适感知系统的鲁棒性和可解释性，为基于LLM的传感器数据分析提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) sensors is a cornerstone of mobile health, smart environments, and human-computer interaction. However, current deep learning-based HAR models often struggle with heavy reliance on labeled data, position-specific ambiguity, and a lack of transparent reasoning. Inspired by the advanced agents framework, which emulates a collaborative agent using Large Language Models (LLMs), we propose SensingAgents, a novel multi-agent system for robust IMU activity recognition. SensingAgents organizes LLM-powered agents into specialized roles: a group of Analyst Agents for position-specific sensor analysis (arm, wrist, belt, pocket), a pair of Advocate Agents that resolves sensor conflicts through dynamic and static dialectical debates, and a Decision Agent that ensures reliability under sensor drift or failure. Evaluation on the Shoaib dataset demonstrates that SensingAgents significantly outperforms state-of-the-art single-agent and multi-agent LLM models, achieving an accuracy of 79.5% in a zero setting--29% higher than existing agent models and 9.4% higher than deep learning baselines--particularly in complex scenarios where multi-sensor data is conflicting or noisy. Our work highlights the potential of multi-agent collaborative reasoning for advancing the robustness and interpretability of ubiquitous sensing systems.

</details>


### [140] [Reward-Decomposed Reinforcement Learning for Immersive Video Role-Playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04733)
*Miao Wang,Yuling Shi,Yijiang Li,Yeheng Chen,Xiaodong Gu,Bin Li,Bo Gao,Yaduan Ruan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EBM-RL框架，通过解耦的GRPO强化学习将角色扮演模型拆解为感知、思考、回答三个阶段，结合四种奖励机制提升场景氛围一致性和角色真实性，在视频驱动的角色扮演对话和VideoQA任务上均取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本角色扮演模型能模仿角色风格，但无法捕捉场景氛围和张力变化，难以满足VR游戏和交互叙事等沉浸式应用的需求。

Method: 提出EBM-RL框架，基于解耦的GRPO方法，将对话生成显式拆分为[感知]（观察视觉线索）、[思考]（内部推理）和[回答]（生成话语）三个阶段；设计了四种互补奖励：CLIP场景-文本对齐奖励、感知-认知奖励、答案准确性奖励和格式奖励。

Result: 在沉浸式角色扮演基准上显著优于纯文本基线和更大规模的视觉语言模型；在视频问答基准上展现出强零样本泛化能力，无需微调即可持续提升性能。

Conclusion: EBM-RL通过显式分离感知-认知-语言生成过程，有效提升了模型对视觉场景的理解和角色扮演的沉浸感，且具备跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Text-based role-playing models can imitate character styles, yet they often fail to reflect a scene's atmosphere and evolving tension, both essential for immersive applications such as Virtual Reality (VR) games and interactive narratives. We study video-grounded role-playing dialogue and introduce EBM-RL (Eye-Brain-Mouth Reinforcement Learning), a decoupled GRPO-based framework that explicitly separates observation ([perception]), reasoning ([think]), and utterance ([answer]). This structure promotes human-like sensory grounding by compelling the model to first attend to visual cues, then form internal interpretations, and finally generate context-appropriate dialogue.
  EBM-RL integrates four complementary rewards: (i) CLIP-based scene-text alignment to improve ambiance and emotion; (ii) a Perceptual-Cognitive reward that encourages [perception] and [think] processes that increase the likelihood of the reference response; (iii) answer accuracy to ensure faithfulness; and (iv) a dense format reward to enforce the desired structured output.
  Extensive experiments demonstrate that EBM-RL substantially outperforms text-only role-playing baselines and larger-scale vision-language models on our immersive role-playing benchmark, delivering simultaneous gains in visual-atmosphere consistency and character authenticity. Beyond the role-playing domain, EBM-RL also exhibits strong zero-shot generalization: without any additional fine-tuning, it consistently improves performance on out-of-domain VideoQA benchmarks. We additionally release an open-source dataset for video-grounded role-playing dialogue.

</details>


### [141] [AgentTrust: Runtime Safety Evaluation and Interception for AI Agent Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04785)
*Chenglin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentTrust 是一个运行时安全层，在智能体工具调用执行前进行拦截并返回结构化裁决（允许/警告/阻止/审查），结合去混淆归一化、SafeFix 安全替代建议、RiskChain 多步攻击链检测和缓存感知的 LLM-as-Judge，在 300 场景内部基准和 630 对抗场景上分别达到 95.0% 和 96.7% 的裁决准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 AI 智能体通过调用工具（文件操作、Shell 命令、HTTP 请求、数据库查询）执行真实世界操作，单个不安全动作可能导致不可逆的损害。现有防御方案存在缺陷：事后基准只能在执行后测量行为、静态防护无法应对混淆和跨步上下文、基础设施沙箱仅约束运行位置而不理解动作含义。

Method: AgentTrust 在工具调用执行前进行拦截并返回结构化裁决。核心组件包括：(1) Shell 去混淆归一化器；(2) SafeFix 安全替代建议；(3) RiskChain 多步攻击链检测；(4) 缓存感知的 LLM-as-Judge 处理歧义输入。

Result: 在 300 场景内部基准（6 个风险类别）上：生产规则集达到 95.0% 裁决准确率、73.7% 风险级别准确率，端到端延迟为低毫秒级。在 630 个独立构建的真实世界对抗场景上：修补规则集下达到 96.7% 裁决准确率，其中 Shell 混淆载荷约 93%。

Conclusion: AgentTrust 提供了一种有效的运行时安全方案，能在低延迟下高准确率地检测和防范智能体工具调用的危险操作，并以 AGPL-3.0 许可证开源，提供 MCP 服务端以支持兼容的智能体。

Abstract: Modern AI agents execute real-world side effects through tool calls such as file operations, shell commands, HTTP requests, and database queries. A single unsafe action, including accidental deletion, credential exposure, or data exfiltration, can cause irreversible harm. Existing defenses are incomplete: post-hoc benchmarks measure behavior after execution, static guardrails miss obfuscation and multi-step context, and infrastructure sandboxes constrain where code runs without understanding what an action means.
  We present AgentTrust, a runtime safety layer that intercepts agent tool calls before execution and returns a structured verdict: allow, warn, block, or review. AgentTrust combines a shell deobfuscation normalizer, SafeFix suggestions for safer alternatives, RiskChain detection for multi-step attack chains, and a cache-aware LLM-as-Judge for ambiguous inputs.
  We release a 300-scenario benchmark across six risk categories and an additional 630 independently constructed real-world adversarial scenarios. On the internal benchmark, the production-only ruleset achieves 95.0% verdict accuracy and 73.7% risk-level accuracy at low-millisecond end-to-end latency. On the 630-scenario benchmark, evaluated under a patched ruleset and not claimed as zero-shot, AgentTrust achieves 96.7% verdict accuracy, including about 93% on shell-obfuscated payloads. AgentTrust is released under the AGPL-3.0 license and provides a Model Context Protocol server for MCP-compatible agents.

</details>


### [142] [DecodingTrust-Agent Platform (DTap): A Controllable and Interactive Red-Teaming Platform for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04808)
*Zhaorun Chen,Xun Liu,Haibo Tong,Chengquan Guo,Yuzhou Nie,Jiawei Zhang,Mintong Kang,Chejian Xu,Qichang Liu,Xiaogeng Liu,Tianneng Shi,Chaowei Xiao,Sanmi Koyejo,Percy Liang,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DecodingTrust-Agent Platform (DTap)，首个可控制、可交互的AI智能体红队测试平台，覆盖14个真实领域和50+模拟环境。同时提出了DTap-Red，首个自主红队测试智能体，可系统探索多种攻击向量并自动发现有效攻击策略。基于此构建了DTap-Bench大规模数据集，并通过大规模评估揭示了主流AI智能体的系统性漏洞模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在复杂长周期工作流中日益广泛部署，但存在严重安全和隐私问题。现有环境缺乏真实、可控、可复现的大规模风险评估平台，难以系统性地评估智能体在面对恶意攻击时的行为。

Method: 构建DTap平台，覆盖14个真实领域和50+模拟环境（如Google Workspace、Paypal、Slack）；提出DTap-Red自主红队测试智能体，能系统探索提示注入、工具注入、技能注入、环境注入及组合攻击等多种向量；构建DTap-Bench大规模数据集，每个实例配有可验证的自动评判器。

Result: 通过DTap对基于多种骨干模型的流行AI智能体进行大规模评估，覆盖安全策略、风险类别和攻击策略，揭示了系统性漏洞模式。

Conclusion: DTap为评估和提升AI智能体的安全性提供了标准化平台和基准，其发现的漏洞模式为开发更安全的下一代智能体提供了重要指导。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly deployed across diverse domains to automate complex workflows through long-horizon and high-stakes action executions. Due to their high capability and flexibility, such agents raise significant security and safety concerns. A growing number of real-world incidents have shown that adversaries can easily manipulate agents into performing harmful actions, such as leaking API keys, deleting user data, or initiating unauthorized transactions. Evaluating agent security is inherently challenging, as agents operate in dynamic, untrusted environments involving external tools, heterogeneous data sources, and frequent user interactions. However, realistic, controllable, and reproducible environments for large-scale risk assessment remain largely underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce the DecodingTrust-Agent Platform (DTap), the first controllable and interactive red-teaming platform for AI agents, spanning 14 real-world domains and over 50 simulation environments that replicate widely used systems such as Google Workspace, Paypal, and Slack. To scale the risk assessment of agents in DTap, we further propose DTap-Red, the first autonomous red-teaming agent that systematically explores diverse injection vectors (e.g., prompt, tool, skill, environment, combinations) and autonomously discovers effective attack strategies tailored to varying malicious goals. Using DTap-Red, we curate DTap-Bench, a large-scale red-teaming dataset comprising high-quality instances across domains, each paired with a verifiable judge to automatically validate attack outcomes. Through DTap, we conduct large-scale evaluations of popular AI agents built on various backbone models, spanning security policies, risk categories, and attack strategies, revealing systematic vulnerability patterns and providing valuable insights for developing secure next-generation agents.

</details>


### [143] [Strat-Reasoner: Reinforcing Strategic Reasoning of LLMs in Multi-Agent Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04906)
*Yidong He,Yutao Lai,Pengxu Yang,Jiarui Gan,Jiexin Wang,Yi Cai,Mengchen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Strat-Reasoner框架，通过递归推理范式、集中式CoT比较模块和群组相对强化学习方法，提升大语言模型在多智能体游戏中的策略推理能力，取得了22.1%的平均性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在单智能体推理任务中表现出色，但在多智能体博弈中面临非平稳性带来的推理评估与信用分配挑战，现有单智能体RL及其多智能体扩展方法未能有效将其他智能体纳入推理过程。

Method: 提出Strat-Reasoner框架：(1) 递归推理范式，将其他智能体的推理过程融入当前智能体的推理中；(2) 集中式思维链(CoT)比较模块，评估中间推理序列质量；(3) 混合优势函数计算与群组相对RL优化方法，提升LLM策略。

Result: 在各种多智能体博弈游戏中，Strat-Reasoner相较基础LLM实现了22.1%的平均性能提升，显著增强了模型的策略推理能力。

Conclusion: 通过将其他智能体的推理纳入递归推理过程，并设计集中式CoT评估与群组相对RL优化，可有效解决多智能体博弈中LLM策略推理的挑战。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel in certain reasoning tasks, they struggle in multi-agent games where the final outcome depends on the joint strategies of all agents. In multi-agent games, the non-stationarity of other agents brings significant challenges on the evaluation of the reasoning process and the credit assignment over multiple reasoning steps. Existing single-agent reinforcement learning (RL) approaches and their multi-agent extensions fail to address these challenges as they do not incorporate other agents in the reasoning process. In this work, we propose Strat-Reasoner, a novel RL-based framework that improves LLMs' strategic reasoning ability in multi-agent games. We introduce a novel recursive reasoning paradigm where an agent's reasoning also integrates other agents' reasoning processes. To provide effective reward signals for the intermediate reasoning sequences, we employ a centralized Chain-of-Thought (CoT) comparison module to evaluate the reasoning quality. Finally, we compute an accurate hybrid advantage and develop a group-relative RL approach to optimize the LLM policy. Experimental results show that Strat-Reasoner substantially improves strategic abilities of underlying LLMs, achieving 22.1\% average performance improvements across various multi-agent games.

</details>


### [144] [A Foundation Model for Zero-Shot Logical Rule Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04916)
*Yin Jun Phua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出神经规则归纳器（NRI），一种用于零样本规则归纳的预训练模型。NRI通过统计编码器和并行槽位解码器，利用领域无关的统计属性（如类条件概率、熵、共现等）表示文字，从而在新任务上无需重新训练即可归纳可解释的逻辑规则。产品T-范数松弛使规则执行可微分，支持端到端训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的归纳逻辑编程方法都是转导式的，其学习参数绑定到特定谓词，每个新任务都需要重新训练，缺乏跨任务的泛化能力。

Method: NRI包含统计编码器和并行槽位解码器。统计编码器用领域无关的统计属性（如类条件概率、熵、共现）表示文字；并行槽位解码器保持逻辑析取的置换不变性。采用产品T-范数松弛使规则执行可微分，实现端到端训练。

Result: 在规则恢复、标签噪声和虚假相关性鲁棒性、以及零样本迁移到真实世界基准任务上进行了评估，取得了良好效果。

Conclusion: 这项工作为符号推理的基础模型开辟了可能性，表明通过统计属性表示文字可以实现跨任务的零样本规则归纳。

Abstract: Inductive Logic Programming (ILP) learns interpretable logical rules from data. Existing methods are transductive: their learned parameters are bound to specific predicates and require retraining for each new task. We introduce Neural Rule Inducer (NRI), a pretrained model for zero-shot rule induction. Rather than encoding literal identities, NRI represents literals using domain-agnostic statistical properties such as class-conditional rates, entropy, and co-occurrence, which generalize across variable identities and counts without retraining. The model consists of a statistical encoder and a parallel slot-based decoder. Parallel decoding preserves the permutation invariance of logical disjunction; an autoregressive decoder would instead impose an arbitrary clause order. Product T-norm relaxation makes rule execution differentiable, allowing end-to-end training on prediction accuracy alone. We evaluate NRI on rule recovery, robustness to label noise and spurious correlations, and zero-shot transfer to real-world benchmarks, and we believe this work opens up the possibility of foundation models for symbolic reasoning. Code and the reference checkpoint are available at https://github.com/phuayj/neural-rule-inducer.

</details>


### [145] [On-line Learning in Tree MDPs by Treating Policies as Bandit Arms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04979)
*Anvay Shah,Ramsundar Anandanarayanan,Sharayu Moharir,Shivaram Kalyanakrishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将树型马尔可夫决策过程（T-MDP）中的在线学习问题转化为基于策略的bandit问题，通过设计基于策略间共享数据的置信界，使得经典的Lucb和Ucb算法能在T-MDP上以多项式级别的内存和每步计算量实现，获得了与终端状态数量相关（而非策略数量）的样本复杂度和遗憾上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T-MDP作为序贯博弈中对抗固定对手决策的抽象模型，其在线学习问题（PAC和遗憾最小化）尚未被充分解决；直接枚举所有策略（数量随状态数指数增长）在计算上不可行。

Method: 将T-MDP的每个策略视为一个臂（arm），应用Lucb和Ucb等经典bandit算法；核心创新在于设计基于策略间共享数据的置信界，使得算法能够以多项式级别的内存和每步计算量实现，避免了直接枚举指数级策略。

Result: 获得了与终端状态数量相关（而非策略数量）的实例依赖的样本复杂度和遗憾上界；在一系列隐藏信息博弈上的实验表明，所提算法持续优于现有替代方案。

Conclusion: 通过巧妙的置信界设计，将T-MDP的在线学习问题转化为可高效求解的bandit问题，在理论和实证层面均验证了有效性。

Abstract: A Tree Markov Decision Problem (T-MDP) is a finite-horizon MDP with a starting state $s_{1}$, in which every state is reachable from $s_{1}$ through exactly one state-action trajectory. T-MDPs arise naturally as abstractions of decision making in sequential games with perfect recall, against stationary opponents. We consider the problem of on-line learning in T-MDPs, both in the PAC and the regret-minimisation regimes. We show that well-known bandit algorithms -- \textsc{Lucb} and \textsc{Ucb} -- can be applied on T-MDPs by treating each policy as an arm. The apparent technical challenge in this approach is that the number of policies is exponential in the number of states. Our main innovation is in the design of confidence bounds based on data shared by the policies, so that the bandit algorithms can yet be implemented with polynomial memory and per-step computation. We obtain instance-dependent upper bounds on sample complexity and regret that sum a ``gap term'' from every terminal state, rather than every policy. Empirically, our algorithms consistently outperform available alternatives on a suite of hidden-information games.

</details>


### [146] [Position: Embodied AI Requires a Privacy-Utility Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05017)
*Xiaoliang Fan,Jiarui Chen,Zhuodong Liu,Ziqi Yang,Peixuan Xu,Ruimin Shen,Junhui Liu,Jianzhong Qi,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了具身AI（EAI）系统的隐私问题是一个生命周期层面的架构约束而非局部特征，并提出了SPINE框架，将隐私视为贯穿EAI全流程的动态控制信号，通过多标准隐私分类矩阵协调跨阶段上下文敏感性，以解决当前各阶段独立优化导致的系统性隐私危机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EAI解决方案在指令、感知、规划和交互等孤立阶段各自取得了进展，但未考虑高频部署中隐私泄露的耦合影响和不可逆性。独立优化各组件在敏感环境下造成了系统性隐私危机，因此需要将隐私视为生命周期级的架构约束。

Method: 提出SPINE（Secure Privacy Integration in Next-generation Embodied AI）统一隐私感知框架，将EAI流水线分解为多个阶段，建立多标准隐私分类矩阵以编排跨阶段边界的上下文敏感性，使隐私作为动态控制信号贯穿整个EAI生命周期。

Result: 通过初步仿真和真实世界案例研究概念性验证了隐私约束如何向下游传播并重塑系统行为，说明了碎片化隐私补丁的不足，并展示了SPINE框架的可行性。

Conclusion: EAI系统的隐私必须是生命周期级的架构设计约束，而非可事后修补的局部特征；SPINE框架为构建安全且功能完整的具身AI系统提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Embodied AI (EAI) systems are rapidly transitioning from simulations into real-world domestic and other sensitive environments. However, recent EAI solutions have largely demonstrated advancements within isolated stages such as instruction, perception, planning and interaction, without considering their coupled privacy implications in high-frequency deployments where privacy leakage is often irreversible. This position paper argues that optimizing these components independently creates a systemic privacy crisis when deployed in sensitive settings, thereby advancing the position that privacy in EAI is a life cycle-level architectural constraint rather than a stage-local feature. To address these challenges, we propose Secure Privacy Integration in Next-generation Embodied AI (SPINE), a unified privacy-aware framework that treats privacy as a dynamic control signal governing cross-stage coupling throughout the entire EAI life cycle. SPINE decomposes the EAI pipeline into various stages and establishes a multi-criterion privacy classification matrix to orchestrate contextual sensitivity across stage boundaries. We conduct preliminary simulation and real-world case studies to conceptually validate how privacy constraints propagate downstream to reshape system behavior, illustrating the insufficiency of fragmented privacy patches and motivating future research directions into secure yet functional embodied AI systems. We detail the SPINE framework and case studies at https://github.com/rminshen03/EAI_Privacy_Position.

</details>


### [147] [LongSeeker: Elastic Context Orchestration for Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05191)
*Yijun Lu,Rui Ye,Yuwen Du,Jiajun Wang,Songhua Liu,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Context-ReAct范式，通过Skip、Compress、Rollback、Snippet、Delete五种原子操作实现自适应上下文管理，并基于此构建了LongSeeker长时搜索智能体，在BrowseComp和BrowseComp-ZH上分别达到61.5%和62.5%的准确率，大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时搜索智能体在推理、调用工具和观察信息过程中，工作上下文会快速增长，简单累积所有中间内容会导致成本增加和错误风险上升，亟需一种自适应的上下文管理方法。

Method: 提出Context-ReAct范式，统一推理、上下文管理和工具使用。定义了五种原子操作：Skip（跳过）、Compress（压缩）、Rollback（回滚）、Snippet（片段提取）和Delete（删除），并证明Compress算子具有表达完备性，其他算子提供效率和保真度保证。基于此范式，使用Qwen3-30B-A3B在1万条合成轨迹上微调得到LongSeeker智能体。

Result: LongSeeker在BrowseComp上达到61.5%，在BrowseComp-ZH上达到62.5%，显著优于Tongyi DeepResearch（43.2%/46.7%）和AgentFold（36.2%/47.3%）。

Conclusion: 自适应上下文管理能够提升长时推理的可靠性和效率，智能体通过主动塑造工作记忆可以实现更优的长时搜索性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon search agents must manage a rapidly growing working context as they reason, call tools, and observe information. Naively accumulating all intermediate content can overwhelm the agent, increasing costs and the risk of errors. We propose that effective context management should be adaptive: parts of the agent's trajectory are maintained at different levels of detail depending on their current relevance to the task. To operationalize this principle, we introduce Context-ReAct, a general agentic paradigm for elastic context orchestration that integrates reasoning, context management, and tool use in a unified loop. Context-ReAct provides five atomic operations: Skip, Compress, Rollback, Snippet and Delete, which allow the agent to dynamically reshape its working context, preserving important evidence, summarizing resolved information, discarding unhelpful branches, and controlling context size. We prove that the Compress operator is expressively complete, while the other specialized operators provide efficiency and fidelity guarantees that reduce generation cost and hallucination risk. Building on this paradigm, we develop LongSeeker, a long-horizon search agent fine-tuned from Qwen3-30B-A3B on 10k synthesized trajectories. Across four representative search benchmarks, LongSeeker achieves 61.5% on BrowseComp and 62.5% on BrowseComp-ZH, substantially outperforming Tongyi DeepResearch (43.2% and 46.7%) and AgentFold (36.2% and 47.3%). These results highlight the potential of adaptive context management, showing that agents can achieve more reliable and efficient long-horizon reasoning by actively shaping their working memory.

</details>
